## Classification and reachability limits

The analyzer records exact AST CallExpr citations, resolves imported functions and receiver-typed method calls, and fails closed for interface dispatch, reflection, generated registration, or runtime configuration. No-caller symbols are `DEAD_OR_UNCERTAIN`; public immediate source files and all downstream target edges are in `call-paths.json`.
